	name = aotsm_demo_c_tie_note

		trigger = { check_variable = { var = aotsm_demo_c_ties value = 1 compare = greater_than } }
		localization_key = aotsm_demo_tied
	}
	text = { localization_key = aotsm_demo_empty }
}
